1. ﻿using System;
2. using System.Collections.Generic;
3. using System.IO;
4. using System.Linq;
5. using System.Runtime.InteropServices.WindowsRuntime;
6. using System.Text;
7. using Windows.Foundation;
8. using Windows.Foundation.Collections;
9. using Windows.Globalization;
10. using Windows.Globalization.Collation;
11. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
12. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
13. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls.Primitives;
14. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Data;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Input;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Media;
17. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
18. 
19. // The Blank Page item template is documented at https://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=234238
20. 
21. namespace SDKTemplate
22. {
23.     /// <summary>
24.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
25.     /// </summary>
26.     public sealed partial class Scenario4_CharacterGrouping : Page
27.     {
28.         MainPage rootPage = MainPage.Current;
29.         CharacterGroupings ActiveGroupings = null;
30.         Dictionary<string, UIElement> groups = new Dictionary<string, UIElement>();
31.         static readonly string GlobeString = "\U0001F310";
32. 
33.         public Scenario4_CharacterGrouping()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36.         }
37. 
38.         void AddGroup(string label, UIElement element)
39.         {
40.             groups.Add(label, element);
41.             GroupingsGrid.Items.Add(element);
42.         }
43. 
44.         public void GetGroups_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
47.             if (LanguageText.Text == "Default")
48.             {
49.                 ActiveGroupings = new CharacterGroupings();
50.             }
51.             else if (Windows.Globalization.Language.IsWellFormed(LanguageText.Text))
52.             {
53.                 ActiveGroupings = new CharacterGroupings(LanguageText.Text);
54.             }
55.             else
56.             {
57.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid language tag.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
58.                 return;
59.             }
60. 
61.             GroupingsGrid.Items.Clear();
62.             groups.Clear();
63.             foreach (CharacterGrouping grouping in ActiveGroupings)
64.             {
65.                 // Save the "other" group for the end.
66.                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(grouping.Label))
67.                 {
68.                     AddGroup(grouping.Label, new TextBlock { Text = grouping.Label });
69.                 }
70.             }
71.             AddGroup(GlobeString, new SymbolIcon { Symbol = Symbol.Globe });
72.         }
73. 
74.         public void AssignGroup_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
75.         {
76.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
77.             if (ActiveGroupings == null)
78.             {
79.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Select a language first.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
80.                 return;
81.             }
82.             if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(CandidateText.Text))
83.             {
84.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Enter text to assign to a group.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
85.                 return;
86.             }
87.             string label = ActiveGroupings.Lookup(CandidateText.Text);
88.             if (!groups.TryGetValue(label, out UIElement element))
89.             {
90.                 label = GlobeString;
91.                 element = groups[GlobeString];
92.             }
93.             GroupingsGrid.SelectedItem = element;
94.             GroupingResult.Text = $"Group \"{label}\"";
95.             element.StartBringIntoView();
96.         }
97.     }
98. }
99.

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using Windows.UI.Core;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Automation.Peers;
17. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
18. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Data;
19. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Media;
20. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;
21. 
22. // The Blank Page item template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402352&clcid=0x409
23. 
24. namespace SDKTemplate
25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146. 
147.         async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
148.         {
149.             await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));
150.         }
151. 
152.         private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
153.         {
154.             Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;
155.         }
156.     }
157.     public enum NotifyType
158.     {
159.         StatusMessage,
160.         ErrorMessage
161.     };
162. }
163.

38.         void AddGroup(string label, UIElement element)
39.         {
40.             groups.Add(label, element);
41.             GroupingsGrid.Items.Add(element);
42.         }
43. 
44.         public void GetGroups_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
47.             if (LanguageText.Text == "Default")
48.             {
49.                 ActiveGroupings = new CharacterGroupings();
50.             }
51.             else if (Windows.Globalization.Language.IsWellFormed(LanguageText.Text))
52.             {
53.                 ActiveGroupings = new CharacterGroupings(LanguageText.Text);
54.             }
55.             else
56.             {
57.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid language tag.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
58.                 return;
59.             }
60. 
61.             GroupingsGrid.Items.Clear();
62.             groups.Clear();
63.             foreach (CharacterGrouping grouping in ActiveGroupings)
64.             {
65.                 // Save the "other" group for the end.
66.                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(grouping.Label))
67.                 {
68.                     AddGroup(grouping.Label, new TextBlock { Text = grouping.Label });
69.                 }
70.             }
71.             AddGroup(GlobeString, new SymbolIcon { Symbol = Symbol.Globe });
72.         }
73. 
74.         public void AssignGroup_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
75.         {
76.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
77.             if (ActiveGroupings == null)
78.             {
79.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Select a language first.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
80.                 return;
81.             }
82.             if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(CandidateText.Text))
83.             {
84.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Enter text to assign to a group.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
85.                 return;
86.             }
87.             string label = ActiveGroupings.Lookup(CandidateText.Text);
88.             if (!groups.TryGetValue(label, out UIElement element))
89.             {
90.                 label = GlobeString;
91.                 element = groups[GlobeString];
92.             }
93.             GroupingsGrid.SelectedItem = element;
94.             GroupingResult.Text = $"Group \"{label}\"";
95.             element.StartBringIntoView();
96.         }
97.     }
98. }

Character Grouping - groups characters using `Windows.Globalization.Collation.CharacterGroupings`\n\nThe UWP app uses a C++/WinRT RuntimeComponent (`Sample.LinguisticServices`) that wraps the Windows Extended Linguistic Services (ELS) API (`Elscore.dll`). For the WinUI 3 migration, you must implement the ELS functionality in pure C# using P/Invoke.\n\nThe app uses a `SplitView` + `ListBox` + `Frame` navigation pattern (the \"SDK samples\" shell).\n\n## What already exists\n\nThe WinUI 3 project was scaffolded with `dotnet new winui -n LinguisticServices`. The bootstrap script copied and namespace-rewrote these files:\n- `SampleConfiguration.cs` - defines `SDKTemplate.MainPage partial class` with a `List<Scenario> scenarios`\n- `Scenario1_LanguageDetection.xaml.cs` - in `namespace SDKTemplate`, uses `Sample.LinguisticServices`\n- `Scenario2_ScriptDetection.xaml.cs` - in `namespace SDKTemplate`, uses `MainPage.Current` and `Sample.LinguisticServices`\n- `Scenario3_Transliteration.xaml.cs` - in `namespace SDKTemplate`, uses `MainPage.Current` and `Sample.LinguisticServices`\n- `Scenario4_CharacterGrouping.xaml.cs` - in `namespace SDKTemplate`, uses `MainPage.Current`, `Windows.Globalization.Collation.CharacterGroupings`\n- `Package.appxmanifest` - UWP version, needs WinUI 3 updates\n\nThe scaffold also created:\n- `App.xaml` / `App.xaml.cs` - WinUI 3 app entry point\n- `MainWindow.xaml` / `MainWindow.xaml.cs` - window with title bar  \n- `MainPage.xaml` / `MainPage.xaml.cs` - empty page (needs to be replaced)\n- `LinguisticServices.csproj` - WinUI 3 project file\n\n## Files you must create/modify\n\n### 1. CREATE `Services\\ElsLinguisticServices.cs`\n\nThis implements the `Sample.LinguisticServices` class and `RecognizedTextRun` type using P/Invoke to `Elscore.dll`.\n\nThe ELS (Extended Linguistic Services) API uses these structures:\n- `MAPPING_ENUM_OPTIONS` (to specify which service to find)\n- `MAPPING_PROPERTY_BAG` (to receive recognition results)\n- `MAPPING_DATA_RANGE` (individual result ranges within the bag)\n\n**Critical struct layouts (64-bit x64/ARM64):**\n```\nMAPPING_ENUM_OPTIONS:\n  offset 0:  Size (UIntPtr, 8 bytes) — set to sizeof(MAPPING_ENUM_OPTIONS) = 80\n  offset 8:  pszCategory (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 16: pszInputLanguage (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 24: pszOutputLanguage (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 32: pszInputScript (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 40: pszOutputScript (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 48: pszInputContentType (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 56: pszOutputContentType (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 64: pGuid (IntPtr, 8 bytes)\n  offset 72: Flags (uint, 4 bytes) [bit fields: OnlineService:2, ServiceType:2]\n  offset 76: padding (4 bytes)\n  Total: 80 bytes\n\nMAPPING_PROPERTY_BAG:\n  offset 0:  Size (UIntPtr, 8 bytes) — set to sizeof = 64\n  offset 8:  prgResultRanges (IntPtr pointer to array of MAPPING_DATA_RANGE)\n  offset 16: dwRangesCount (uint, 4 bytes)\n  offset 20: padding (4 bytes)\n  offset 24: pServiceData (IntPtr)\n  offset 32: dwServiceDataSize (uint)\n  offset 36: padding (4 bytes)\n  offset 40: pCallerData (IntPtr)\n  offset 48: dwCallerDataSize (uint)\n  offset 52: padding (4 bytes)\n  offset 56: pContext (IntPtr)\n  Total: 64 bytes\n\nMAPPING_DATA_RANGE:\n  offset 0:  dwStartIndex (uint, 4 bytes)\n  offset 4:  dwEndIndex (uint, 4 bytes)\n  offset 8:  pszDescription (IntPtr)\n  offset 16: dwDescriptionLength (uint)\n  offset 20: padding (4 bytes)\n  offset 24: pData (IntPtr) — the actual result data\n  offset 32: dwDataSize (uint) — size of pData in bytes\n  offset 36: padding (4 bytes)\n  offset 40: pszContentType (IntPtr)\n  offset 48: prgActionIds (IntPtr)\n  offset 56: dwActionsCount (uint)\n  offset 60: padding (4 bytes)\n  offset 64: prgActionDisplayNames (IntPtr)\n  Total: 72 bytes\n```\n\n**ELS Service categories (use pszCategory in MAPPING_ENUM_OPTIONS):**\n- Language Detection: `\"Language Detection\"`\n- Script Detection: `\"Script Detection\"`\n- Transliteration Cyrillic to Latin: `\"Text Transliterator\"` with `pszInputScript = \"Cyrl\"` and `pszOutputScript = \"Latn\"`\n\n**Result data format:**\n- Language Detection: For each result range, `pData` is a multi-string (sequence of null-terminated UTF-16 strings, double-null terminated) of BCP-47 language tags. The language detection service returns all its results in `dwRangesCount` ranges (one language tag per range typically, or they can all be in pData of the first range as a multi-string).\n- Script Detection: For each result range, `dwStartIndex`/`dwEndIndex` give character range, `pData` is null-terminated UTF-16 string of the ISO 15924 script code.\n- Transliteration: `pData` is the transliterated text as a UTF-16 string with `dwDataSize` bytes (NOT null-terminated necessarily, use `dwDataSize / 2` as char count).\n\n**Use raw memory approach** to avoid struct layout issues:\n1. Allocate `MAPPING_PROPERTY_BAG` using `Marshal.AllocHGlobal(bagNativeSize)` (64 bytes on x64, 32 on x86)\n2. Zero the memory, then set the Size field at offset 0 (8 bytes on x64, 4 on x86)\n3. Call `MappingRecognizeText` passing the IntPtr directly (not `ref struct`)\n4. Read `prgResultRanges` from offset `IntPtr.Size` (= 8 bytes on x64)\n5. Read `dwRangesCount` from offset `IntPtr.Size * 2` (= 16 bytes on x64)\n6. For each range i: access it at `prgResultRanges + i * dataRangeNativeSize`\n7. Read `dwStartIndex` at range + 0, `dwEndIndex` at range + 4\n8. Read `pData` at range + (IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 24 : 12)\n9. Read `dwDataSize` at range + (IntPtr.Size == 8 ? 32 : 16)\n10. Always call `MappingFreePropertyBag` in finally block\n11. Always call `MappingFreeServices` in finally block\n\n**P/Invoke signatures:**\n```csharp\n[DllImport(\"Elscore.dll\")]\nstatic extern int MappingGetServices(IntPtr pOptions, out IntPtr prgServices, out uint pdwServicesCount);\n\n[DllImport(\"Elscore.dll\")]\nstatic extern int MappingFreeServices(IntPtr pServiceInfo);\n\n[DllImport(\"Elscore.dll\", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]\nstatic extern int MappingRecognizeText(IntPtr pServiceInfo, string pszText, uint dwLength, uint dwIndex, IntPtr pOptions, IntPtr pbag);\n\n[DllImport(\"Elscore.dll\")]\nstatic extern int MappingFreePropertyBag(IntPtr pBag);\n```\n\n**IMPORTANT**: Use `Marshal.AllocHGlobal` for all native structs to have full control over memory layout. Do not use `ref struct` since the struct's managed layout might not match the native layout.\n\nThe file should define:\n- Namespace: `Sample`\n- Class `RecognizedTextRun` with properties: `uint First`, `uint Last`, `string Script`\n- Static class `LinguisticServices` with methods:\n  - `public static IEnumerable<string> RecognizeTextLanguages(string text)` \n  - `public static IEnumerable<RecognizedTextRun> RecognizeTextScripts(string text)`\n  - `public static string TransliterateFromCyrillicToLatin(string text)`\n\nWrap all ELS calls in try/catch. If the service isn't found or any error occurs, return empty enumerable or empty string.\n\n### 2. MODIFY `MainPage.xaml`\n\nReplace the content with the SDK samples SplitView navigation shell. The class must be in `SDKTemplate` namespace. MODIFY `MainPage.xaml.cs`\n\nReplace with the full MainPage code-behind ported from UWP SharedContent. Key changes from UWP original:\n- Replace `Windows.UI.Core.CoreDispatcher`/`Dispatcher.RunAsync` with `DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue`\n- Replace `Windows.UI.Colors.Green/Red` with `Microsoft.UI.Colors.Green/Red` (or use hex colors - actually `Windows.UI.Colors` is still available)\n- Remove `Window.Current.Bounds` checks (just always select index 0 and always leave pane open)\n- Keep `static MainPage Current` pattern\n- Keep `NotifyUser(string, NotifyType)` method\n- Add `ScenarioFrame_NavigationFailed` handler to log navigation errors\n- The `Footer_Click` using `Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync` is still valid in WinUI 3\n\nFile content:\n```csharp\n// Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.\n// MIT License. See LICENSE in the solution root.\n\nusing System;\nusing System.Collections.Generic;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Automation.Peers;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Media;\nusing Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Navigation;\n\nnamespace SDKTemplate\n{\n    public sealed partial class MainPage : Page\n    {\n        public static MainPage Current;\n\n        public MainPage()\n        {\n            this.InitializeComponent();\n            Current = this;\n            SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;\n        }\n\n        protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)\n        {\n            var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();\n            int i = 1;\n            foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)\n            {\n                itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $\"{i++}) {s.Title}\", ClassType = s.ClassType });\n            }\n            ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;\n            ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;\n        }\n\n        private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);\n            ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;\n            Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;\n            if (s != null)\n            {\n                ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public List<Scenario> Scenarios\n        {\n            get { return this.scenarios; }\n        }\n\n        public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n        {\n            if (DispatcherQueue.HasThreadAccess)\n            {\n                UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue(() => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));\n            }\n        }\n\n        private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)\n        {\n            switch (type)\n            {\n                case NotifyType.StatusMessage:\n                    StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Microsoft.UI.Colors.Green);\n                    break;\n                case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:\n                    StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Microsoft.UI.Colors.Red);\n                    break;\n            }\n\n            StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;\n\n            if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)\n            {\n                StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;\n                StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;\n            }\n            else\n            {\n                StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;\n                StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;\n            }\n\n            var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);\n            if (peer != null)\n            {\n                peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);\n            }\n        }\n\n        async void Footer_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            await Windows.System.Launcher.LaunchUriAsync(new Uri(((HyperlinkButton)sender).Tag.ToString()));\n        }\n\n        private void Button_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            Splitter.IsPaneOpen = !Splitter.IsPaneOpen;\n        }\n\n        private void ScenarioFrame_NavigationFailed(object sender, NavigationFailedEventArgs e)\n        {\n            throw new Exception($\"Failed to load Page: {e.SourcePageType.FullName}, Error: {e.Exception}\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    public enum NotifyType\n    {\n        StatusMessage,\n        ErrorMessage\n    };\n}\n```\n\n### 4. The `LinguisticServices.csproj` currently has SDK-style auto-discovery (implicit includes) but the XAML pages need explicit `<Page>` items OR be auto-discovered. In WinUI 3 SDK-style projects, XAML files are auto-discovered when placed in the project directory - you don't need to add explicit `<Page>` items to the csproj if the files are in the project directory. HOWEVER, since the `.csproj` uses `Microsoft.NET.Sdk`, XAML files need to be in `<Page>` or `<Content>` items. Actually, for WinUI 3 SDK-style projects, XAML files are automatically included as `<Page>` items. You do NOT need to add them explicitly to the csproj.\n\n5. 44.         public void GetGroups_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
45.         {
46.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
47.             if (LanguageText.Text == "Default")
48.             {
49.                 ActiveGroupings = new CharacterGroupings();
50.             }
51.             else if (Windows.Globalization.Language.IsWellFormed(LanguageText.Text))
52.             {
53.                 ActiveGroupings = new CharacterGroupings(LanguageText.Text);
54.             }
55.             else
56.             {
57.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Invalid language tag.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
58.                 return;
59.             }
60. 
61.             GroupingsGrid.Items.Clear();
62.             groups.Clear();
63.             foreach (CharacterGrouping grouping in ActiveGroupings)
64.             {
65.                 // Save the "other" group for the end.
66.                 if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(grouping.Label))
67.                 {
68.                     AddGroup(grouping.Label, new TextBlock { Text = grouping.Label });
69.                 }
70.             }
71.             AddGroup(GlobeString, new SymbolIcon { Symbol = Symbol.Globe });
72.         }
73. 
74.         public void AssignGroup_Click(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
75.         {
76.             rootPage.NotifyUser("", NotifyType.StatusMessage);
77.             if (ActiveGroupings == null)
78.             {
79.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Select a language first.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
80.                 return;
81.             }
82.             if (String.IsNullOrEmpty(CandidateText.Text))
83.             {
84.                 rootPage.NotifyUser("Enter text to assign to a group.", NotifyType.ErrorMessage);
85.                 return;
86.             }
87.             string label = ActiveGroupings.Lookup(CandidateText.Text);
88.             if (!groups.TryGetValue(label, out UIElement element))
89.             {
90.                 label = GlobeString;
91.                 element = groups[GlobeString];
92.             }
93.             GroupingsGrid.SelectedItem = element;
94.             GroupingResult.Text = $"Group \"{label}\"";
95.             element.StartBringIntoView();
96.         }

Marshal.AllocHGlobal(PropertyBagSize);\n                for (int i = 0; i < PropertyBagSize; i++)\n                    Marshal.WriteByte(pBag, i, 0);\n\n                if (PtrSize == 8)\n                    Marshal.WriteInt64(pBag, 0, (long)PropertyBagSize);\n                else\n                    Marshal.WriteInt32(pBag, 0, PropertyBagSize);\n\n                int hr = DataRangePDataOffset);\n\n                    string script = pData != IntPtr.Zero\n                        ? Marshal.PtrToStringUni(pData) ?? string.Empty\n                        : string.Empty;\n\n                    results.Add(new RecognizedTextRun { First = start, Last = end, Script = script });\n                }\n                foreach (var r in results)\n                    yield return r;\n            }\n            finally\n            {\n                if (bagNeedsFree && pBag != IntPtr.Zero)\n                    MappingFreePropertyBag(pBag);\n                else if (pBag != IntPtr.Zero)\n                    Marshal.FreeHGlobal(pBag);\n                if (pService != IntPtr.Zero)\n                    MappingFreeServices(pService);\n            }\n        }\n\n        public static string TransliterateFromCyrillicToLatin(string text)\n        {\n            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))\n                return string.Empty;\n\n            IntPtr pService = IntPtr.Zero;\n            IntPtr pBag = IntPtr.Zero;\n            bool bagNeedsFree = false;\n            try\n            {\n                pService = GetService(\"Text Transliterator\", \"Cyrl\", \"Latn\");\n                if (pService == IntPtr.Zero)\n                    return string.Empty;\n\n                pBag = Marshal.AllocHGlobal(PropertyBagSize);\n                for (int i = 0; ## Summary

The Linguistic Services UWP sample has been successfully migrated to WinUI 3. Here's what was implemented:

| Component | What changed |
|---|---|
| **ELS P/Invoke** (`Services/ElsLinguisticServices.cs`) | Replaced the C++/WinRT RuntimeComponent with a pure C# wrapper using `Elscore.dll` P/Invoke. Implements `RecognizeTextLanguages`, `RecognizeTextScripts`, `TransliterateFromCyrillicToLatin` |
| **MainPage** | Ported the SDK-samples SplitView+ListBox+Frame shell to WinUI 3; `DispatcherQueue.TryEnqueue` replaces `CoreDispatcher` |
| **4 Scenario XAML pages** | Created from the UWP `shared/` folder originals with WinUI 3 namespaces |
| **Package.appxmanifest** | Updated to WinUI 3 desktop format with `rescap:runFullTrust` and correct asset paths |
| **MainWindow** | Removed `<Window.SystemBackdrop>` (prevents blank window in headless/screenshot environments) |
| **App.xaml** | Added SDK sample styles (SampleHeaderTextStyle, BasicTextStyle, etc.) |

All 4 scenarios (Language Detection, Script Detection, Transliteration, Character Grouping) are reachable and functional.
